![thumbnail-image](https://yt3.ggpht.com/K9C5Cw5w8eJExAdIpdi2Xs9MqnR2tSDh4LGZi8d6kAygCgQyhgNILs9yPcS-IVDKIY6NG8ZB=s68-c-k-c0x00ffffff-no-rj)

World Labs8.03K subscribers

### Create 3D worlds from a simple prompt

Marble lets you create, edit and share high-fidelity, persistent 3D worlds. Whether you're an artist, designer, engineer or just curious, our powerful yet intuitive tools make it easy to bring your vision to life.

### Key features

- Explore



Discover worlds created by the community and get inspired

- Create



Generate new worlds from text, images, videos or 3D structures

- Worlds



Browse, download or export all the worlds you've created

- Studio



Experiment with advanced tools for editing, stitching multiple worlds together and creating cinematic videos of your worlds


**Pro Tip:** For detailed tutorials and guides, check out our learning resources [here](https://docs.worldlabs.ai/).

Get started
